Siiniion R. Sangma sagi Chejing R. Marakni de> 
pante ong'achim. Uni ma'sagini songara Rongrokgre ong'- 
chim. Ua 1S95 bilsio Nokmakundi ( Dainadubi Thanani* 
samba ) soogo atchiaha. Uni abitang sakgni Node aro Reje 
ong'acbim. Ma'gipa siani jamano Reje R. Sangma uko 
aldu-mitangrikaha. Ua Primary skulko matchote Nisban- 
gram Upper Primary skulo porae 1910 bilsio matchotaha. 
Ua 1911 bilsio Turaona re'bae Middle English skulko mat- 
chote ua scholarsbipko man’e 1913 bisio Goalpara High 
skulo poraeaha. Unoni 19141 bilsio Shillong Government 
High skulona transfer ra'ange uno porae 1917 bilsio mat- 
chotaha. Ua 1918 bisio jagitamna Tura Middle English 
skulo masterni kamko ka'aha. Ua 1919 bisini January 
jao Smti. Mohini R. Marako bia ka'aha. Ua bilsi 1920 
ni March Jaoni Tura Middle English skulo (jean dao Govern- 
ment M. P. skul) masterni kamko ka'aha. Ua bilsi 1951 
November jao kamko ( Pension man'aha ). wataha.

Ua kam ka'miting somirango toremni kamnaba gisik 
nange mondolini kamrangkoba ka'paha. Da'o dongen- 
gipa Tura monoloini bipek Rongkhongre songona rcan- 
gronge skiprakaniko dakrongaba. Bilsi 1927-1928-0 mon- 
dolini Deacon-ni kamkoba ka'aha. Pension man'ani jamano 
ua Garo National Councilni dolo nap'e jatna kamko ka'na 
gita bilsi 1957-0 Memberna seokako man'e Education, Civil 
Works aro Agriculture Departmentni Executive Memberna 
seokako man'e . kamko ka'aha. Ua an'tangni bil 
ama dipet ka'e 1964 bilsioba memberna seoka ko'man*- 
taeaba. Ua an'tangni man'ginin kamko ka'engon beeni an-

TODIK ARO UNI MA GIPA
